Normally I really love painting, or just wandering through museum exhibits. But recently... I just leave my art supplies sitting there untouched. I don't really have anything else to add to that, it's just... yeah, I'm not enjoying much of anything lately.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any changes in your appetite or weight recently?\n\nPatient: Yeah, I have. And I've noticed my skirts for work are fitting a little looser lately, even though I haven't been trying to lose weight at all.\n\nDoctor: Have you found yourself feeling guilty or blaming yourself for things more than usual, even if they don’t seem like a big deal to others?\n\nPatient: Yes, I have. I just keep... replaying things in my head a lot lately. Like every missed family plan, or every tense conversation I've had with my husband. I just feel like I’m letting everyone down right now—as a wife, a mother, and even at work as a manager.
